Split queries into batches for worker processes\n    2. Each worker performs cosine similarity search on its batch\n    3. Collect and format results\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (list of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count() or 1\n\n    # Split queries into batches\n    queries_per_worker = max(1, len(queries) // num_workers)\n    query_batches = [\n        queries[i : i + queries_per_worker]\n        for i in range(0, len(queries), queries_per_worker)\n    ]\n\n    # Prepare arguments for worker batches\n    worker_args = [\n        (\n            batch,\n            index.idf,\n            index.inverted_index,\n            index.doc_vectors,\n            index.doc_norms,\n            top_k,\n        )\n        for batch in query_batches\n    ]\n\n    results_list = []\n    with mp.Pool(num_workers) as pool:\n        batch_results = pool.starmap(_search_worker_batch, worker_args)\n        # Flatten batch results\n        for batch in batch_results:\n            results_list.extend(batch)\n\n    # Convert to SearchResult objects\n    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n\n    final_results = []\n    for raw_results in results_list:\n        query_results = []\n        for doc_id, score in raw_results:\n            query_results.append(\n                SearchResult(\n                    doc_id=doc_id,\n                    score=score,\n                    title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\"),\n                )\n            )\n        final_results.append(query_results)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return final_results, elapsed","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nParallel TF-IDF Implementation\n\nThis module provides a parallel implementation of:\n1. TF-IDF index building using multiprocessing\n2. Batch search using multiprocessing\n3. Identical results to sequential implementation\n\nPerformance targets:\n- 1.5x speedup for index building with 4 workers\n- 2x speedup for batch search with 4 workers\n\"\"\"\n\nimport argparse\nimport math\nimport multiprocessing as mp\nimport os\nimport re\nimport time\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\nfrom heapq import nlargest\nfrom typing import Optional\n\nfrom document_generator import Document, generate_corpus, load_corpus\nfrom sequential import (\n    STOP_WORDS,\n    TOKEN_PATTERN,\n    IndexingResult,\n    SearchResult,\n    TFIDFIndex,\n    compute_term_frequencies,\n    tokenize,\n)\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from parallel index building.\"\"\"\n\n    index: TFIDFIndex\n    elapsed_time: float\n    num_documents: int\n    vocabulary_size: int\n\n\ndef _tokenize_and_compute_tf_batch(docs: list[Document]) -> list[tuple[int, dict[str, float], set[str]]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function to tokenize a batch of documents and compute TF.\n    Returns: list of (doc_id, tf_dict, unique_terms_set)\n    \"\"\"\n    results = []\n    for doc in docs:\n        text = doc.title + \" \" + doc.content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        results.append((doc.doc_id, tf, set(tf.keys())))\n    return results\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: list[Document],\n    num_workers: Optional[int] = None,\n    chunk_size: int = 500,\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel.\n\n    Strategy:\n    1. Parallelize tokenization and TF computation across workers\n    2. Collect results and compute document frequencies\n    3. Compute IDF scores\n    4. Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores\n    5. Compute document vector norms\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Documents per batch for worker pool\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count() or 1\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Step 1: Parallelize tokenization and TF computation\n    doc_term_freqs: dict[int, dict[str, float]] = {}\n    doc_terms: dict[int, set[str]] = {}\n    all_terms = set()\n\n    # Split documents into batches for workers\n    doc_batches = [\n        documents[i : i + chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(documents), chunk_size)\n    ]\n\n    with mp.Pool(num_workers) as pool:\n        batch_results = pool.map(_tokenize_and_compute_tf_batch, doc_batches)\n        for batch in batch_results:\n            for doc_id, tf_dict, terms_set in batch:\n                doc_term_freqs[doc_id] = tf_dict\n                doc_terms[doc_id] = terms_set\n                all_terms.update(terms_set)\n\n    index.vocabulary = all_terms\n\n    # Step 2: Calculate document frequencies\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        index.document_frequencies[term] = df\n\n    # Step 3: Compute IDF scores\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 4: Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        posting_list = []\n        for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n            if term in tf_dict:\n                tf = tf_dict[term]\n                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n        posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        index.inverted_index[term] = posting_list\n\n    # Step 5: Build document vectors and compute norms\n    for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n        index.doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        index.doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary),\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _search_worker_batch(\n    queries: list[str],\n    idf: dict[str, float],\n    inverted_index: dict[str, list[tuple[int, float]]],\n    doc_vectors: dict[int, dict[str, float]],\n    doc_norms: dict[int, float],\n    top_k: int,\n) -> list[list[tuple[int, float]]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function to search for a batch of queries.\n    Returns list of result lists, each containing (doc_id, score) tuples.\n    \"\"\"\n    results = []\n    for query in queries:\n        # Tokenize query\n        query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n        if not query_tokens:\n            results.append([])\n            continue\n\n        # Compute query TF\n        query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n        # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n        query_vector = {}\n        query_norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n            if term in idf:\n                tfidf = tf * idf[term]\n                query_vector[term] = tfidf\n                query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n        if not query_vector:\n            results.append([])\n            continue\n\n        query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n        # Find candidate documents\n        candidate_docs = set()\n        for term in query_vector:\n            if term in inverted_index:\n                for doc_id, _ in inverted_index[term]:\n                    candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n        # Compute cosine similarity for each candidate\n        scores = []\n        for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n            doc_vector = doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n            doc_norm = doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n            if doc_norm == 0:\n                continue\n\n            # Dot product\n            dot_product = sum(\n                query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n                for term in query_vector\n            )\n\n            # Cosine similarity\n            similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n            scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n        # Get top-k results\n        top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n        results.append(top_results)\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: Optional[int] = None,\n    documents: Optional[list[Document]] = None,\n) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Strategy:\n    1. Distribute queries across worker processes\n    2. Each worker performs cosine similarity search\n    3. Collect and format results\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (list of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count() or 1\n\n    # Use starmap to pass both query and fixed parameters\n    search_args = [(query, index, top_k) for query in queries]\n\n    results_list = []\n    with mp.Pool(num_workers) as pool:\n        raw_results = pool.starmap(_search_worker, search_args)\n        results_list = raw_results\n\n    # Convert to SearchResult objects\n    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n\n    final_results = []\n    for raw_results in results_list:\n        query_results = []\n        for doc_id, score in raw_results:\n            query_results.append(\n                SearchResult(\n                    doc_id=doc_id,\n                    score=score,\n                    title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\"),\n                )\n            )\n        final_results.append(query_results)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return final_results, elapsed\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Entry Point\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef main():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Parallel TF-IDF indexing and search\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--corpus\", type=str, default=None, help=\"Path to corpus JSON file\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--num-docs\", type=int, default=5000, help=\"Number of documents to generate if no corpus provided\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--query\", type=str, default=\"machine learning algorithm\", help=\"Search query\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--top-k\", type=int, default=10, help=\"Number of top results to return\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--num-workers\", type=int, default=None, help=\"Number of worker processes\")\n\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Parallel TF-IDF Search Engine\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Load or generate corpus\n    if args.corpus:\n        print(f\"\\nLoading corpus from {args.corpus}...\")\n        documents = load_corpus(args.corpus)\n    else:\n        print(f\"\\nGenerating {args.num_docs} documents...\")\n        documents = generate_corpus(args.num_docs, seed=42)\n\n    print(f\"Corpus size: {len(documents)} documents\")\n    total_words = sum(d.word_count for d in documents)\n    print(f\"Total words: {total_words:,}\")\n\n    # Build index\n    print(\"\\nBuilding TF-IDF index in parallel...\")\n    result = build_tfidf_index_parallel(documents, num_workers=args.num_workers)\n\n    print(f\"\\nIndex built in {result.elapsed_time:.3f} seconds\")\n    print(f\"Vocabulary size: {result.vocabulary_size:,} terms\")\n    print(f\"Documents indexed: {result.num_documents}\")\n\n    # Perform search\n    print(f\"\\nSearching for: '{args.query}'\")\n    print(\"-\" * 60)\n\n    results, search_time = batch_search_parallel(\n        [args.query],\n        result.index,\n        args.top_k,\n        num_workers=args.num_workers,\n        documents=documents,\n    )\n\n    print(f\"Search completed in {search_time*1000:.2f} ms\")\n    print(f\"\\nTop {len(results[0])} results:\")\n    for i, res in enumerate(results[0], 1):\n        print(f\"  {i}. [{res.score:.4f}] {res.title} (doc_id: {res.doc_id})\")\n\n    return result\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":243,"oldLines":8,"newStart":243,"newLines":8,"lines":["     Search for multiple queries in parallel."," ","     Strategy:","-    1. TF-IDF index building using multiprocessing\n2. Batch search using multiprocessing\n3. Identical results to sequential implementation\n\nPerformance targets:\n- 1.5x speedup for index building with 4 workers\n- 2x speedup for batch search with 4 workers\n\"\"\"\n\nimport argparse\nimport math\nimport multiprocessing as mp\nimport os\nimport re\nimport time\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\nfrom heapq import nlargest\nfrom typing import Optional\n\nfrom document_generator import Document, generate_corpus, load_corpus\nfrom sequential import (\n    STOP_WORDS,\n    TOKEN_PATTERN,\n    IndexingResult,\n    SearchResult,\n    TFIDFIndex,\n    compute_term_frequencies,\n    tokenize,\n)\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from parallel index building.\"\"\"\n\n    index: TFIDFIndex\n    elapsed_time: float\n    num_documents: int\n    vocabulary_size: int\n\n\ndef _tokenize_and_compute_tf_batch(docs: list[Document]) -> list[tuple[int, dict[str, float], set[str]]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function to tokenize a batch of documents and compute TF.\n    Returns: list of (doc_id, tf_dict, unique_terms_set)\n    \"\"\"\n    results = []\n    for doc in docs:\n        text = doc.title + \" \" + doc.content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        results.append((doc.doc_id, tf, set(tf.keys())))\n    return results\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: list[Document],\n    num_workers: Optional[int] = None,\n    chunk_size: int = 500,\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel.\n\n    Strategy:\n    1. Parallelize tokenization and TF computation across workers\n    2. Collect results and compute document frequencies\n    3. Compute IDF scores\n    4. Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores\n    5. Compute document vector norms\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Documents per batch for worker pool\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count() or 1\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Step 1: Parallelize tokenization and TF computation\n    doc_term_freqs: dict[int, dict[str, float]] = {}\n    doc_terms: dict[int, set[str]] = {}\n    all_terms = set()\n\n    # Split documents into batches for workers\n    doc_batches = [\n        documents[i : i + chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(documents), chunk_size)\n    ]\n\n    with mp.Pool(num_workers) as pool:\n        batch_results = pool.map(_tokenize_and_compute_tf_batch, doc_batches)\n        for batch in batch_results:\n            for doc_id, tf_dict, terms_set in batch:\n                doc_term_freqs[doc_id] = tf_dict\n                doc_terms[doc_id] = terms_set\n                all_terms.update(terms_set)\n\n    index.vocabulary = all_terms\n\n    # Step 2: Calculate document frequencies\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        index.document_frequencies[term] = df\n\n    # Step 3: Compute IDF scores\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 4: Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        posting_list = []\n        for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n            if term in tf_dict:\n                tf = tf_dict[term]\n                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n        posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        index.inverted_index[term] = posting_list\n\n    # Step 5: Build document vectors and compute norms\n    for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n        index.doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        index.doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary),\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _search_worker_batch(\n    queries: list[str],\n    idf: dict[str, float],\n    inverted_index: dict[str, list[tuple[int, float]]],\n    doc_vectors: dict[int, dict[str, float]],\n    doc_norms: dict[int, float],\n    top_k: int,\n) -> list[list[tuple[int, float]]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function to search for a batch of queries.\n    Returns list of result lists, each containing (doc_id, score) tuples.\n    \"\"\"\n    results = []\n    for query in queries:\n        # Tokenize query\n        query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n        if not query_tokens:\n            results.append([])\n            continue\n\n        # Compute query TF\n        query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n        # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n        query_vector = {}\n        query_norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n            if term in idf:\n                tfidf = tf * idf[term]\n                query_vector[term] = tfidf\n                query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n        if not query_vector:\n            results.append([])\n            continue\n\n        query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n        # Find candidate documents\n        candidate_docs = set()\n        for term in query_vector:\n            if term in inverted_index:\n                for doc_id, _ in inverted_index[term]:\n                    candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n        # Compute cosine similarity for each candidate\n        scores = []\n        for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n            doc_vector = doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n            doc_norm = doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n            if doc_norm == 0:\n                continue\n\n            # Dot product\n            dot_product = sum(\n                query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n                for term in query_vector\n            )\n\n            # Cosine similarity\n            similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n            scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n        # Get top-k results\n        top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n        results.append(top_results)\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: Optional[int] = None,\n    documents: Optional[list[Document]] = None,\n) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Strategy:\n    1. Split queries into batches for worker processes\n    2. Each worker performs cosine similarity search on its batch\n    3. Collect and format results\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (list of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count() or 1\n\n    # Split queries into batches\n    queries_per_worker = max(1, len(queries) // num_workers)\n    query_batches = [\n        queries[i : i + queries_per_worker]\n        for i in range(0, len(queries), queries_per_worker)\n    ]\n\n    # Prepare arguments for worker batches\n    worker_args = [\n        (\n            batch,\n            index.idf,\n            index.inverted_index,\n            index.doc_vectors,\n            index.doc_norms,\n            top_k,\n        )\n        for batch in query_batches\n    ]\n\n    results_list = []\n    with mp.Pool(num_workers) as pool:\n        batch_results = pool.starmap(_search_worker_batch, worker_args)\n        # Flatten batch results\n        for batch in batch_results:\n            results_list.extend(batch)\n\n    # Convert to SearchResult objects\n    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n\n    final_results = []\n    for raw_results in results_list:\n        query_results = []\n        for doc_id, score in raw_results:\n            query_results.append(\n                SearchResult(\n                    doc_id=doc_id,\n                    score=score,\n                    title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\"),\n                )\n            )\n        final_results.append(query_results)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return final_results, elapsed\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Entry Point\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef main():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Parallel TF-IDF indexing and search\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--corpus\", type=str, default=None, help=\"Path to corpus JSON file\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--num-docs\", type=int, default=5000, help=\"Number of documents to generate if no corpus provided\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--query\", type=str, default=\"machine learning algorithm\", help=\"Search query\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--top-k\", type=int, default=10, help=\"Number of top results to return\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--num-workers\", type=int, default=None, help=\"Number of worker processes\")\n\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Parallel TF-IDF Search Engine\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Load or generate corpus\n    if args.corpus:\n        print(f\"\\nLoading corpus from {args.corpus}...\")\n        documents = load_corpus(args.corpus)\n    else:\n        print(f\"\\nGenerating {args.num_docs} documents...\")\n        documents = generate_corpus(args.num_docs, seed=42)\n\n    print(f\"Corpus size: {len(documents)} documents\")\n    total_words = sum(d.word_count for d in documents)\n    print(f\"Total words: {total_words:,}\")\n\n    # Build index\n    print(\"\\nBuilding TF-IDF index in parallel...\")\n    result = build_tfidf_index_parallel(documents, num_workers=args.num_workers)\n\n    print(f\"\\nIndex built in {result.elapsed_time:.3f} seconds\")\n    print(f\"Vocabulary size: {result.vocabulary_size:,} terms\")\n    print(f\"Documents indexed: {result.num_documents}\")\n\n    # Perform search\n    print(f\"\\nSearching for: '{args.query}'\")\n    print(\"-\" * 60)\n\n    results, search_time = batch_search_parallel(\n        [args.query],\n        result.index,\n        args.top_k,\n        num_workers=args.num_workers,\n        documents=documents,\n    )\n\n    print(f\"Search completed in {search_time*1000:.2f} ms\")\n    print(f\"\\nTop {len(results[0])} results:\")\n    for i, res in enumerate(results[0], 1):\n        print(f\"  {i}. TF-IDF index building using multiprocessing\n2. Batch search using multiprocessing\n3. Identical results to sequential implementation\n\nPerformance targets:\n- 1.5x speedup for index building with 4 workers\n- 2x speedup for batch search with 4 workers\n\"\"\"\n\nimport argparse\nimport math\nimport multiprocessing as mp\nimport os\nimport re\nimport time\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\nfrom heapq import nlargest\nfrom typing import Optional\n\nfrom document_generator import Document, generate_corpus, load_corpus\nfrom sequential import (\n    STOP_WORDS,\n    TOKEN_PATTERN,\n    IndexingResult,\n    SearchResult,\n    TFIDFIndex,\n    compute_term_frequencies,\n    tokenize,\n)\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from parallel index building.\"\"\"\n\n    index: TFIDFIndex\n    elapsed_time: float\n    num_documents: int\n    vocabulary_size: int\n\n\ndef _tokenize_and_compute_tf_batch(docs: list[Document]) -> list[tuple[int, dict[str, float], set[str]]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function to tokenize a batch of documents and compute TF.\n    Returns: list of (doc_id, tf_dict, unique_terms_set)\n    \"\"\"\n    results = []\n    for doc in docs:\n        text = doc.title + \" \" + doc.content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        results.append((doc.doc_id, tf, set(tf.keys())))\n    return results\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: list[Document],\n    num_workers: Optional[int] = None,\n    chunk_size: int = 500,\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel.\n\n    Strategy:\n    1. Parallelize tokenization and TF computation across workers\n    2. Collect results and compute document frequencies\n    3. Compute IDF scores\n    4. Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores\n    5. Compute document vector norms\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Documents per batch for worker pool\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count() or 1\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Step 1: Parallelize tokenization and TF computation\n    doc_term_freqs: dict[int, dict[str, float]] = {}\n    doc_terms: dict[int, set[str]] = {}\n    all_terms = set()\n\n    # Split documents into batches for workers\n    doc_batches = [\n        documents[i : i + chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(documents), chunk_size)\n    ]\n\n    with mp.Pool(num_workers) as pool:\n        batch_results = pool.map(_tokenize_and_compute_tf_batch, doc_batches)\n        for batch in batch_results:\n            for doc_id, tf_dict, terms_set in batch:\n                doc_term_freqs[doc_id] = tf_dict\n                doc_terms[doc_id] = terms_set\n                all_terms.update(terms_set)\n\n    index.vocabulary = all_terms\n\n    # Step 2: Calculate document frequencies\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        index.document_frequencies[term] = df\n\n    # Step 3: Compute IDF scores\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 4: Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        posting_list = []\n        for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n            if term in tf_dict:\n                tf = tf_dict[term]\n                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n        posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        index.inverted_index[term] = posting_list\n\n    # Step 5: Build document vectors and compute norms\n    for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n        index.doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        index.doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary),\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _search_worker_batch(\n    queries: list[str],\n    idf: dict[str, float],\n    inverted_index: dict[str, list[tuple[int, float]]],\n    doc_vectors: dict[int, dict[str, float]],\n    doc_norms: dict[int, float],\n    top_k: int,\n) -> list[list[tuple[int, float]]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function to search for a batch of queries.\n    Returns list of result lists, each containing (doc_id, score) tuples.\n    \"\"\"\n    results = []\n    for query in queries:\n        # Tokenize query\n        query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n        if not query_tokens:\n            results.append([])\n            continue\n\n        # Compute query TF\n        query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n        # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n        query_vector = {}\n        query_norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n            if term in idf:\n                tfidf = tf * idf[term]\n                query_vector[term] = tfidf\n                query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n        if not query_vector:\n            results.append([])\n            continue\n\n        query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n        # Find candidate documents\n        candidate_docs = set()\n        for term in query_vector:\n            if term in inverted_index:\n                for doc_id, _ in inverted_index[term]:\n                    candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n        # Compute cosine similarity for each candidate\n        scores = []\n        for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n            doc_vector = doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n            doc_norm = doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n            if doc_norm == 0:\n                continue\n\n            # Dot product\n            dot_product = sum(\n                query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n                for term in query_vector\n            )\n\n            # Cosine similarity\n            similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n            scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n        # Get top-k results\n        top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n        results.append(top_results)\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: Optional[int] = None,\n    documents: Optional[list[Document]] = None,\n) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Strategy:\n    1. Split queries into batches for worker processes\n    2. Each worker performs cosine similarity search on its batch\n    3. Collect and format results\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (list of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count() or 1\n\n    # Split queries into batches\n    queries_per_worker = max(1, len(queries) // num_workers)\n    query_batches = [\n        queries[i : i + queries_per_worker]\n        for i in range(0, len(queries), queries_per_worker)\n    ]\n\n    # Prepare arguments for worker batches\n    worker_args = [\n        (\n            batch,\n            index.idf,\n            index.inverted_index,\n            index.doc_vectors,\n            index.doc_norms,\n            top_k,\n        )\n        for batch in query_batches\n    ]\n\n    results_list = []\n    with mp.Pool(num_workers) as pool:\n        batch_results = pool.starmap(_search_worker_batch, worker_args)\n        # Flatten batch results\n        for batch in batch_results:\n            results_list.extend(batch)\n\n    # Convert to SearchResult objects\n    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n\n    final_results = []\n    for raw_results in results_list:\n        query_results = []\n        for doc_id, score in raw_results:\n            query_results.append(\n                SearchResult(\n                    doc_id=doc_id,\n                    score=score,\n                    title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\"),\n                )\n            )\n        final_results.append(query_results)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return final_results, elapsed\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Entry Point\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef main():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Parallel TF-IDF indexing and search\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--corpus\", type=str, default=None, help=\"Path to corpus JSON file\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--num-docs\", type=int, default=5000, help=\"Number of documents to generate if no corpus provided\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--query\", type=str, default=\"machine learning algorithm\", help=\"Search query\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--top-k\", type=int, default=10, help=\"Number of top results to return\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--num-workers\", type=int, default=None, help=\"Number of worker processes\")\n\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Parallel TF-IDF Search Engine\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Load or generate corpus\n    if args.corpus:\n        print(f\"\\nLoading corpus from {args.corpus}...\")\n        documents = load_corpus(args.corpus)\n    else:\n        print(f\"\\nGenerating {args.num_docs} documents...\")\n        documents = generate_corpus(args.num_docs, seed=42)\n\n    print(f\"Corpus size: {len(documents)} documents\")\n    total_words = sum(d.word_count for d in documents)\n    print(f\"Total words: {total_words:,}\")\n\n    # Build index\n    print(\"\\nBuilding TF-IDF index in parallel...\")\n    result = build_tfidf_index_parallel(documents, num_workers=args.num_workers)\n\n    print(f\"\\nIndex built in {result.elapsed_time:.3f} seconds\")\n    print(f\"Vocabulary size: {result.vocabulary_size:,} terms\")\n    print(f\"Documents indexed: {result.num_documents}\")\n\n    # Perform search\n    print(f\"\\nSearching for: '{args.query}'\")\n    print(\"-\" * 60)\n\n    results, search_time = batch_search_parallel(\n        [args.query],\n        result.index,\n        args.top_k,\n        num_workers=args.num_workers,\n        documents=documents,\n    )\n\n    print(f\"Search completed in {search_time*1000:.2f} ms\")\n    print(f\"\\nTop {len(results[0])} results:\")\n    for i, res in enumerate(results[0], 1):\n        print(f\"  {i}. [{res.score:.4f}] {res.title} (doc_id: {res.doc_id})\")\n\n    return result\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":162,"oldLines":77,"newStart":162,"newLines":71,"lines":[" # Collect and format results\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker threads (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (list of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count() or 1\n\n    # Use ThreadPoolExecutor for shared memory access (no pickling overhead)\n    results_list = []\n    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        futures = [\n            executor.submit(\n                _search_single_query,\n                query,\n                index.idf,\n                index.inverted_index,\n                index.doc_vectors,\n                index.doc_norms,\n                top_k,\n            )\n            for query in queries\n        ]\n        for future in futures:\n            results_list.append(future.result())\n\n    # Convert to SearchResult objects\n    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n\n    final_results = []\n    for raw_results in results_list:\n        query_results = []\n        for doc_id, score in raw_results:\n            query_results.append(\n                SearchResult(\n                    doc_id=doc_id,\n                    score=score,\n                    title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\"),\n                )\n            )\n        final_results.append(query_results)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return final_results, elapsed","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nParallel TF-IDF Implementation\n\nThis module provides a parallel implementation of:\n1. TF-IDF index building using multiprocessing\n2. Batch search using multiprocessing\n3. Identical results to sequential implementation\n\nPerformance targets:\n- 1.5x speedup for index building with 4 workers\n- 2x speedup for batch search with 4 workers\n\"\"\"\n\nimport argparse\nimport math\nimport multiprocessing as mp\nimport os\nimport re\nimport time\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\nfrom heapq import nlargest\nfrom typing import Optional\n\nfrom document_generator import Document, generate_corpus, load_corpus\nfrom sequential import (\n    STOP_WORDS,\n    TOKEN_PATTERN,\n    IndexingResult,\n    SearchResult,\n    TFIDFIndex,\n    compute_term_frequencies,\n    tokenize,\n)\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from parallel index building.\"\"\"\n\n    index: TFIDFIndex\n    elapsed_time: float\n    num_documents: int\n    vocabulary_size: int\n\n\ndef _tokenize_and_compute_tf_batch(docs: list[Document]) -> list[tuple[int, dict[str, float], set[str]]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function to tokenize a batch of documents and compute TF.\n    Returns: list of (doc_id, tf_dict, unique_terms_set)\n    \"\"\"\n    results = []\n    for doc in docs:\n        text = doc.title + \" \" + doc.content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        results.append((doc.doc_id, tf, set(tf.keys())))\n    return results\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: list[Document],\n    num_workers: Optional[int] = None,\n    chunk_size: int = 500,\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel.\n\n    Strategy:\n    1. Parallelize tokenization and TF computation across workers\n    2. Collect results and compute document frequencies\n    3. Compute IDF scores\n    4. Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores\n    5. Compute document vector norms\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Documents per batch for worker pool\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count() or 1\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Step 1: Parallelize tokenization and TF computation\n    doc_term_freqs: dict[int, dict[str, float]] = {}\n    doc_terms: dict[int, set[str]] = {}\n    all_terms = set()\n\n    # Split documents into batches for workers\n    doc_batches = [\n        documents[i : i + chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(documents), chunk_size)\n    ]\n\n    with mp.Pool(num_workers) as pool:\n        batch_results = pool.map(_tokenize_and_compute_tf_batch, doc_batches)\n        for batch in batch_results:\n            for doc_id, tf_dict, terms_set in batch:\n                doc_term_freqs[doc_id] = tf_dict\n                doc_terms[doc_id] = terms_set\n                all_terms.update(terms_set)\n\n    index.vocabulary = all_terms\n\n    # Step 2: Calculate document frequencies\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        index.document_frequencies[term] = df\n\n    # Step 3: Compute IDF scores\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 4: Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        posting_list = []\n        for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n            if term in tf_dict:\n                tf = tf_dict[term]\n                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n        posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        index.inverted_index[term] = posting_list\n\n    # Step 5: Build document vectors and compute norms\n    for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n        index.doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        index.doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary),\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _search_single_query(\n    query: str,\n    idf: dict[str, float],\n    inverted_index: dict[str, list[tuple[int, float]]],\n    doc_vectors: dict[int, dict[str, float]],\n    doc_norms: dict[int, float],\n    top_k: int,\n) -> list[tuple[int, float]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Perform search for a single query.\n    Returns list of (doc_id, score) tuples.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in idf:\n            tfidf = tf * idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Find candidate documents\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    # Compute cosine similarity for each candidate\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Dot product\n        dot_product = sum(\n            query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n            for term in query_vector\n        )\n\n        # Cosine similarity\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n    return top_results\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: Optional[int] = None,\n    documents: Optional[list[Document]] = None,\n) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Strategy:\n    1. Split queries into batches for worker processes\n    2. Each worker performs cosine similarity search on its batch\n    3. Collect and format results\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (list of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count() or 1\n\n    # Split queries into batches\n    queries_per_worker = max(1, len(queries) // num_workers)\n    query_batches = [\n        queries[i : i + queries_per_worker]\n        for i in range(0, len(queries), queries_per_worker)\n    ]\n\n    # Prepare arguments for worker batches\n    worker_args = [\n        (\n            batch,\n            index.idf,\n            index.inverted_index,\n            index.doc_vectors,\n            index.doc_norms,\n            top_k,\n        )\n        for batch in query_batches\n    ]\n\n    results_list = []\n    with mp.Pool(num_workers) as pool:\n        batch_results = pool.starmap(_search_worker_batch, worker_args)\n        # Flatten batch results\n        for batch in batch_results:\n            results_list.extend(batch)\n\n    # Convert to SearchResult objects\n    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n\n    final_results = []\n    for raw_results in results_list:\n        query_results = []\n        for doc_id, score in raw_results:\n            query_results.append(\n                SearchResult(\n                    doc_id=doc_id,\n                    score=score,\n                    title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\"),\n                )\n            )\n        final_results.append(query_results)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return final_results, elapsed\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Entry Point\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef main():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Parallel TF-IDF indexing and search\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--corpus\", type=str, default=None, help=\"Path to corpus JSON file\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--num-docs\", type=int, default=5000, help=\"Number of documents to generate if no corpus provided\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--query\", type=str, default=\"machine learning algorithm\", help=\"Search query\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--top-k\", type=int, default=10, help=\"Number of top results to return\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--num-workers\", type=int, default=None, help=\"Number of worker processes\")\n\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Parallel TF-IDF Search Engine\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Load or generate corpus\n    if args.corpus:\n        print(f\"\\nLoading corpus from {args.corpus}...\")\n        documents = load_corpus(args.corpus)\n    else:\n        print(f\"\\nGenerating {args.num_docs} documents...\")\n        documents = generate_corpus(args.num_docs, seed=42)\n\n    print(f\"Corpus size: {len(documents)} documents\")\n    total_words = sum(d.word_count for d in documents)\n    print(f\"Total words: {total_words:,}\")\n\n    # Build index\n    print(\"\\nBuilding TF-IDF index in parallel...\")\n    result = build_tfidf_index_parallel(documents, num_workers=args.num_workers)\n\n    print(f\"\\nIndex built in {result.elapsed_time:.3f} seconds\")\n    print(f\"Vocabulary size: {result.vocabulary_size:,} terms\")\n    print(f\"Documents indexed: {result.num_documents}\")\n\n    # Perform search\n    print(f\"\\nSearching for: '{args.query}'\")\n    print(\"-\" * 60)\n\n    results, search_time = batch_search_parallel(\n        [args.query],\n        result.index,\n        args.top_k,\n        num_workers=args.num_workers,\n        documents=documents,\n    )\n\n    print(f\"Search completed in {search_time*1000:.2f} ms\")\n    print(f\"\\nTop {len(results[0])} results:\")\n    for i, res in enumerate(results[0], 1):\n        print(f\"  {i}. [{res.score:.4f}] {res.title} (doc_id: {res.doc_id})\")\n\n    return result\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":235,"oldLines":18,"newStart":235,"newLines":18,"lines":["     documents: Optional[list[Document]] = None,"," ) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:","     \"\"\"","-    Search for multiple queries in parallel.","+    Search for multiple queries in parallel using threading."," ","     Strategy:","-    1. Parallelize tokenization and TF computation across workers\n    2. Collect results and compute document frequencies\n    3. Compute IDF scores\n    4. Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores\n    5. TF-IDF index building using multiprocessing\n2. Batch search using multiprocessing\n3. Identical results to sequential implementation\n\nPerformance targets:\n- 1.5x speedup for index building with 4 workers\n- 2x speedup for batch search with 4 workers\n\"\"\"\n\nimport argparse\nimport math\nimport multiprocessing as mp\nimport os\nimport re\nimport time\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\nfrom heapq import nlargest\nfrom typing import Optional\n\nfrom document_generator import Document, generate_corpus, load_corpus\nfrom sequential import (\n    STOP_WORDS,\n    TOKEN_PATTERN,\n    IndexingResult,\n    SearchResult,\n    TFIDFIndex,\n    compute_term_frequencies,\n    tokenize,\n)\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from parallel index building.\"\"\"\n\n    index: TFIDFIndex\n    elapsed_time: float\n    num_documents: int\n    vocabulary_size: int\n\n\ndef _tokenize_and_compute_tf_batch(docs: list[Document]) -> list[tuple[int, dict[str, float], set[str]]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function to tokenize a batch of documents and compute TF.\n    Returns: list of (doc_id, tf_dict, unique_terms_set)\n    \"\"\"\n    results = []\n    for doc in docs:\n        text = doc.title + \" \" + doc.content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        results.append((doc.doc_id, tf, set(tf.keys())))\n    return results\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: list[Document],\n    num_workers: Optional[int] = None,\n    chunk_size: int = 500,\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel.\n\n    Strategy:\n    1. Parallelize tokenization and TF computation across workers\n    2. Collect results and compute document frequencies\n    3. Compute IDF scores\n    4. Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores\n    5. Compute document vector norms\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Documents per batch for worker pool\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count() or 1\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Step 1: Parallelize tokenization and TF computation\n    doc_term_freqs: dict[int, dict[str, float]] = {}\n    doc_terms: dict[int, set[str]] = {}\n    all_terms = set()\n\n    # Split documents into batches for workers\n    doc_batches = [\n        documents[i : i + chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(documents), chunk_size)\n    ]\n\n    with mp.Pool(num_workers) as pool:\n        batch_results = pool.map(_tokenize_and_compute_tf_batch, doc_batches)\n        for batch in batch_results:\n            for doc_id, tf_dict, terms_set in batch:\n                doc_term_freqs[doc_id] = tf_dict\n                doc_terms[doc_id] = terms_set\n                all_terms.update(terms_set)\n\n    index.vocabulary = all_terms\n\n    # Step 2: Calculate document frequencies\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        index.document_frequencies[term] = df\n\n    # Step 3: Compute IDF scores\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 4: Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        posting_list = []\n        for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n            if term in tf_dict:\n                tf = tf_dict[term]\n                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n        posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        index.inverted_index[term] = posting_list\n\n    # Step 5: Build document vectors and compute norms\n    for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n        index.doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        index.doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary),\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _search_single_query(\n    query: str,\n    idf: dict[str, float],\n    inverted_index: dict[str, list[tuple[int, float]]],\n    doc_vectors: dict[int, dict[str, float]],\n    doc_norms: dict[int, float],\n    top_k: int,\n) -> list[tuple[int, float]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Perform search for a single query.\n    Returns list of (doc_id, score) tuples.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in idf:\n            tfidf = tf * idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Find candidate documents\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    # Compute cosine similarity for each candidate\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Dot product\n        dot_product = sum(\n            query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n            for term in query_vector\n        )\n\n        # Cosine similarity\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n    return top_results\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: Optional[int] = None,\n    documents: Optional[list[Document]] = None,\n) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel using threading.\n\n    Strategy:\n    1. Use ThreadPoolExecutor to parallelize query processing\n    2. Each thread performs cosine similarity search\n    3. Collect and format results\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker threads (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (list of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count() or 1\n\n    # Use ThreadPoolExecutor for shared memory access (no pickling overhead)\n    results_list = []\n    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        futures = [\n            executor.submit(\n                _search_single_query,\n                query,\n                index.idf,\n                index.inverted_index,\n                index.doc_vectors,\n                index.doc_norms,\n                top_k,\n            )\n            for query in queries\n        ]\n        for future in futures:\n            results_list.append(future.result())\n\n    # Convert to SearchResult objects\n    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n\n    final_results = []\n    for raw_results in results_list:\n        query_results = []\n        for doc_id, score in raw_results:\n            query_results.append(\n                SearchResult(\n                    doc_id=doc_id,\n                    score=score,\n                    title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\"),\n                )\n            )\n        final_results.append(query_results)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return final_results, elapsed\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Entry Point\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef main():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Parallel TF-IDF indexing and search\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--corpus\", type=str, default=None, help=\"Path to corpus JSON file\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--num-docs\", type=int, default=5000, help=\"Number of documents to generate if no corpus provided\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--query\", type=str, default=\"machine learning algorithm\", help=\"Search query\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--top-k\", type=int, default=10, help=\"Number of top results to return\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--num-workers\", type=int, default=None, help=\"Number of worker processes\")\n\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Parallel TF-IDF Search Engine\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Load or generate corpus\n    if args.corpus:\n        print(f\"\\nLoading corpus from {args.corpus}...\")\n        documents = load_corpus(args.corpus)\n    else:\n        print(f\"\\nGenerating {args.num_docs} documents...\")\n        documents = generate_corpus(args.num_docs, seed=42)\n\n    print(f\"Corpus size: {len(documents)} documents\")\n    total_words = sum(d.word_count for d in documents)\n    print(f\"Total words: {total_words:,}\")\n\n    # Build index\n    print(\"\\nBuilding TF-IDF index in parallel...\")\n    result = build_tfidf_index_parallel(documents, num_workers=args.num_workers)\n\n    print(f\"\\nIndex built in {result.elapsed_time:.3f} seconds\")\n    print(f\"Vocabulary size: {result.vocabulary_size:,} terms\")\n    print(f\"Documents indexed: {result.num_documents}\")\n\n    # Perform search\n    print(f\"\\nSearching for: '{args.query}'\")\n    print(\"-\" * 60)\n\n    results, search_time = batch_search_parallel(\n        [args.query],\n        result.index,\n        args.top_k,\n        num_workers=args.num_workers,\n        documents=documents,\n    )\n\n    print(f\"Search completed in {search_time*1000:.2f} ms\")\n    print(f\"\\nTop {len(results[0])} results:\")\n    for i, res in enumerate(results[0], 1):\n        print(f\"  {i}. [{res.score:.4f}] {res.title} (doc_id: {res.doc_id})\")\n\n    return result\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":92,"oldLines":6,"newStart":92,"newLines":11,"lines":["     if num_workers is None:","         num_workers = os.cpu_count() or 1"," ","+    # Increase chunk size to reduce pickling overhead","+    effective_chunk_size = max(chunk_size, 200)","+    if len(documents) < 2000:","+        effective_chunk_size = max(effective_chunk_size, len(documents) // (num_workers * 2))","+","     index = TFIDFIndex()","     index.num_documents = len(documents)"," "]},{"oldStart":102,"oldLines":7,"newStart":107,"newLines":8,"lines":[" ","     # Split documents into batches for workers","     doc_batches = [","-        documents[i : i + chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(documents), chunk_size)","+        documents[i : i + effective_chunk_size]","+        for i in range(0, len(documents), effective_chunk_size)","     ]"," ","     with mp.Pool(num_workers) as pool:"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

TF-IDF index building using multiprocessing\n2. Batch search using multiprocessing\n3. Identical results to sequential implementation\n\nPerformance targets:\n- 1.5x speedup for index building with 4 workers\n- 2x speedup for batch search with 4 workers\n\"\"\"\n\nimport argparse\nimport math\nimport multiprocessing as mp\nimport os\nimport re\nimport time\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\nfrom heapq import nlargest\nfrom typing import Optional\n\nfrom document_generator import Document, generate_corpus, load_corpus\nfrom sequential import (\n    STOP_WORDS,\n    TOKEN_PATTERN,\n    IndexingResult,\n    SearchResult,\n    TFIDFIndex,\n    compute_term_frequencies,\n    tokenize,\n)\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from parallel index building.\"\"\"\n\n    index: TFIDFIndex\n    elapsed_time: float\n    num_documents: int\n    vocabulary_size: int\n\n\ndef _tokenize_and_compute_tf_batch(docs: list[Document]) -> list[tuple[int, dict[str, float], set[str]]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function to tokenize a batch of documents and compute TF.\n    Returns: list of (doc_id, tf_dict, unique_terms_set)\n    \"\"\"\n    results = []\n    for doc in docs:\n        text = doc.title + \" \" + doc.content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        results.append((doc.doc_id, tf, set(tf.keys())))\n    return results\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: list[Document],\n    num_workers: Optional[int] = None,\n    chunk_size: int = 500,\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel.\n\n    Strategy:\n    1. Parallelize tokenization and TF computation across workers\n    2. Collect results and compute document frequencies\n    3. Compute IDF scores\n    4. Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores\n    5. Compute document vector norms\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Documents per batch for worker pool\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count() or 1\n\n    # Increase chunk size to reduce pickling overhead\n    effective_chunk_size = max(chunk_size, 200)\n    if len(documents) < 2000:\n        effective_chunk_size = max(effective_chunk_size, len(documents) // (num_workers * 2))\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Step 1: Parallelize tokenization and TF computation\n    doc_term_freqs: dict[int, dict[str, float]] = {}\n    doc_terms: dict[int, set[str]] = {}\n    all_terms = set()\n\n    # Split documents into batches for workers\n    doc_batches = [\n        documents[i : i + effective_chunk_size]\n        for i in range(0, len(documents), effective_chunk_size)\n    ]\n\n    with mp.Pool(num_workers) as pool:\n        batch_results = pool.map(_tokenize_and_compute_tf_batch, doc_batches)\n        for batch in batch_results:\n            for doc_id, tf_dict, terms_set in batch:\n                doc_term_freqs[doc_id] = tf_dict\n                doc_terms[doc_id] = terms_set\n                all_terms.update(terms_set)\n\n    index.vocabulary = all_terms\n\n    # Step 2: Calculate document frequencies\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        index.document_frequencies[term] = df\n\n    # Step 3: Compute IDF scores\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 4: Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        posting_list = []\n        for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n            if term in tf_dict:\n                tf = tf_dict[term]\n                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n        posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        index.inverted_index[term] = posting_list\n\n    # Step 5: Build document vectors and compute norms\n    for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n        index.doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        index.doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary),\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _search_single_query(\n    query: str,\n    idf: dict[str, float],\n    inverted_index: dict[str, list[tuple[int, float]]],\n    doc_vectors: dict[int, dict[str, float]],\n    doc_norms: dict[int, float],\n    top_k: int,\n) -> list[tuple[int, float]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Perform search for a single query.\n    Returns list of (doc_id, score) tuples.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in idf:\n            tfidf = tf * idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Find candidate documents\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    # Compute cosine similarity for each candidate\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Dot product\n        dot_product = sum(\n            query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n            for term in query_vector\n        )\n\n        # Cosine similarity\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n    return top_results\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: Optional[int] = None,\n    documents: Optional[list[Document]] = None,\n) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel using threading.\n\n    Strategy:\n    1. Use ThreadPoolExecutor to parallelize query processing\n    2. Each thread performs cosine similarity search\n    3. Collect and format results\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker threads (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (list of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count() or 1\n\n    # Use ThreadPoolExecutor for shared memory access (no pickling overhead)\n    results_list = []\n    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        futures = [\n            executor.submit(\n                _search_single_query,\n                query,\n                index.idf,\n                index.inverted_index,\n                index.doc_vectors,\n                index.doc_norms,\n                top_k,\n            )\n            for query in queries\n        ]\n        for future in futures:\n            results_list.append(future.result())\n\n    # Convert to SearchResult objects\n    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n\n    final_results = []\n    for raw_results in results_list:\n        query_results = []\n        for doc_id, score in raw_results:\n            query_results.append(\n                SearchResult(\n                    doc_id=doc_id,\n                    score=score,\n                    title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\"),\n                )\n            )\n        final_results.append(query_results)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return final_results, elapsed\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Entry Point\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef main():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Parallel TF-IDF indexing and search\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--corpus\", type=str, default=None, help=\"Path to corpus JSON file\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--num-docs\", type=int, default=5000, help=\"Number of documents to generate if no corpus provided\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--query\", type=str, default=\"machine learning algorithm\", help=\"Search query\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--top-k\", type=int, default=10, help=\"Number of top results to return\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--num-workers\", type=int, default=None, help=\"Number of worker processes\")\n\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Parallel TF-IDF Search Engine\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Load or generate corpus\n    if args.corpus:\n        print(f\"\\nLoading corpus from {args.corpus}...\")\n        documents = load_corpus(args.corpus)\n    else:\n        print(f\"\\nGenerating {args.num_docs} documents...\")\n        documents = generate_corpus(args.num_docs, seed=42)\n\n    print(f\"Corpus size: {len(documents)} documents\")\n    total_words = sum(d.word_count for d in documents)\n    print(f\"Total words: {total_words:,}\")\n\n    # Identical results to sequential implementation\n\nPerformance targets:\n- 1.5x speedup for index building with 4 workers\n- 2x speedup for batch search with 4 workers\n\"\"\"\n\nimport argparse\nimport math\nimport multiprocessing as mp\nimport os\nimport re\nimport time\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\nfrom heapq import nlargest\nfrom typing import Optional\n\nfrom document_generator import Document, generate_corpus, load_corpus\nfrom sequential import (\n    STOP_WORDS,\n    TOKEN_PATTERN,\n    IndexingResult,\n    SearchResult,\n    TFIDFIndex,\n    compute_term_frequencies,\n    tokenize,\n)\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from parallel index building.\"\"\"\n\n    index: TFIDFIndex\n    elapsed_time: float\n    num_documents: int\n    vocabulary_size: int\n\n\ndef _tokenize_and_compute_tf_batch(docs: list[Document]) -> list[tuple[int, dict[str, float], set[str]]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function to tokenize a batch of documents and compute TF.\n    Returns: list of (doc_id, tf_dict, unique_terms_set)\n    \"\"\"\n    results = []\n    for doc in docs:\n        text = doc.title + \" \" + doc.content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        results.append((doc.doc_id, tf, set(tf.keys())))\n    return results\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: list[Document],\n    num_workers: Optional[int] = None,\n    chunk_size: int = 500,\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel.\n\n    Strategy:\n    1. Parallelize tokenization and TF computation across workers\n    2. Collect results and compute document frequencies\n    3. Compute IDF scores\n    4. Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores\n    5. num_workers = os.cpu_count() or 1\n\n    # Increase chunk size to reduce pickling overhead\n    effective_chunk_size = max(chunk_size, 200)\n    if len(documents) < 2000:\n        effective_chunk_size doc_term_freqs: dict[int, dict[str, float]] = {}\n    doc_terms: dict[int, set[str]] = {}\n    all_terms = set()\n\n    # Split documents into batches for workers\n    doc_batches = [\n        documents[i : i + effective_chunk_size]\n        for i in range(0, len(documents), effective_chunk_size)\n    ]\n\n    with mp.Pool(num_workers) as pool:\n        batch_results =            doc_terms[doc_id] = terms_set\n                all_terms.update(terms_set)\n\n    index.vocabulary = all_terms\n\n    # Step 2: Calculate document frequencies\n    def _compute_df_for_terms(terms_list: list[str]) -> dict[str, int]:\n        \"\"\"Helper to compute document frequencies for a batch of terms.\"\"\"\n        result = {}\n        for term in terms_list:\n            df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n            result[term] = df\n        return result\n\n    # Parallelize DF computation\n    terms_list = list(index.vocabulary)\n    terms_per_batch = max(1, len(terms_list) // num_workers)\n    term_batches = [\n        terms_list[i : i + terms_per_batch]\n        for i in range(0, len(terms_list), terms_per_batch)\n    ]\n\n    with mp.Pool(num_workers) as pool:\n        batch_dfs = pool.map(_compute_df_for_terms, term_batches)\n        for batch_df in batch_dfs:\n            index.document_frequencies.update(batch_df)\n\n    # Step 3: Compute IDF scores\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 4: Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores\n    def _build_inverted_index_batch(\n        terms_batch: list[str],\n    ) -> dict[str, list[tuple[int, float]]]:\n        \"\"\"Helper to build inverted index for a batch of terms.\"\"\"\n        result = {}\n        for term in terms_batch:\n            posting_list = []\n            for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n                if term in tf_dict:\n                    tf = tf_dict[term]\n                    tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                    posting_list.append((doc_id, _search_single_query(\n    query: str,\n    idf: dict[str, float],\n    inverted_index: dict[str, list[tuple[int, float]]],\n    doc_vectors: dict[int, dict[str, float]],\n    doc_norms: dict[int,
